                                                          subtree_text: Pylint is not trusting your typing and is inferring the actual values of nodes (for a start because there was no typing when pylint started off) using its internal code representation (astroid). If your code is import logging as argparse , Pylint can check and know that argparse.error(...) is in fact a logging call and not an argparse call. This makes pylint slower, but it also lets pylint find more issues if your code is not fully typed.
